- [Project Structure](#project-structure)
- [Commands](#commands)
- [Code Conventions](#code-conventions)

- [Audit Trail](#audit-trail)

## Project Structure
- Python backend: `app/` (FastAPI + SQLAlchemy 2.0 + Pydantic v2) — **Python >=3.13,<3.15**
- TypeScript frontend: `frontend/src/` (React 19 + Vite 8/Rolldown + TanStack Router + TanStack Query + Zustand + Framer Motion)
- Rust optimizer: `native/rust_ext/` (PyO3 FFI — schedule conflicts, partition management, HMAC)
- Go services: `services/` (ws-hub, file-processor, gateway, caddy)
- Alembic migrations: `alembic/versions/` (112 files; squash script: `app/management/squash_migrations.py`)
- Config: `app/core/config/` (15 classes, _NamespaceView composition Phase 2, 174+ fields)
- Feature flags: `app/core/feature_flags.py` (OpenFeature + flagd; K8s config: `k8s/flagd/`)
- Observability: OTEL tracing (Tempo), OTEL log bridge, Sentry, Prometheus, Pyroscope

## Commands
- `python -m ruff check app/` — lint backend (S104/S105 suppressed via per-file-ignores)
- `python -m ruff format app/` — format backend
- `cd frontend && npx tsc --noEmit` — typecheck frontend
- `python -m py_compile <file>` — quick syntax check
- Pre-commit runs: ruff, ruff-format, detect-secrets, bandit, mypy
- After detect-secrets hook: `git add .secrets.baseline` (always re-stage)

## Code Conventions
- Commit style: `feat(waveXX): description` with `Co-Authored-By` trailer
- Branch: `egorribun`
- Exception handling: narrowed to specific types with `# RZ-20-04` / `# RZ-22-01` audit comments
  - DB/network: `(OSError, ConnectionError)`
  - File ops: `(FileNotFoundError, OSError)`
  - Redis: `(ConnectionError, TimeoutError, OSError)`
  - PyO3/Rust: `(RuntimeError, ImportError, OSError)`
  - SMTP: `(OSError, smtplib.SMTPException)`
  - Keep broad `except Exception` only for: re-raise-after-cleanup, convert-to-domain, handler-nak, fail-closed auth
  - ALL broad catches must be tagged: `# RZ-22-01-JUSTIFIED: <reason>` (Wave 22 completed: 147 justified, 29 narrowed)
  - Python 2 except syntax: always use `except (A, B):` tuple form, NEVER `except A, B:` (convention enforced since Wave 23)
- Models: ALL relationships must have explicit `lazy="noload"` — prevent N+1
- Settings: use `@cached_property` namespace accessors (settings.db, settings.security, etc.)
  - Phase 2 (Wave 21): accessors return `_NamespaceView` proxies, not `self`
- Frontend validation: **Valibot only** (Zod removed in Wave 21)
- Frontend debounce: `useDebounced` from `@/hooks/useDebounced` — strategy presets: `"search"` (200ms), `"default"` (300ms), `"validation"` (350ms) (PERF-23-04, Wave 23)
- Frontend sanitization fallback: SafeHtml strips HTML tags on WASM failure — never renders nothing (RZ-24-04)
- Frontend memo: `React.memo()` on list/grid/dashboard components
- GraphQL: 5 defense layers — QueryDepthLimiter, MaxTokensLimiter, QueryCostExtension, RequestTimeoutExtension, PersistedQueryExtension (prod only)
- Feature flags: `from app.core.feature_flags import is_enabled` (async) or `is_enabled_sync`
- Password hashing: Argon2id only — **bcrypt verification removed** (TD-21-04, Wave 21)
- Valkey eviction: `volatile-lru` (changed from allkeys-lru in Wave 21 — RZ-21-02)
- OTEL Metrics: bridge via `PrometheusMetricReader` in `configure_metrics()` — new metrics use OTEL API, legacy use prometheus_client (MOD-23-05)
- Suspense queries: `useSuspenseMyEventsQuery` for components inside `<Suspense>` — do NOT use for offline-fallback hooks (MOD-23-02)
- Bundle budget: main JS chunk must be <500 KB (enforced in CI via bundle-analysis job, MOD-23-06)
- L1 cache metrics: `cache_l1_hits_total`, `cache_l1_misses_total` Prometheus counters (TD-30-05)
- CI: `relationship(` without `lazy=` rejected by MOD-30-01 gate — use `# noload-exempt: <reason>` for exceptions
- Gateway: startup errors use channel-based propagation, not `os.Exit` — defers always execute (RZ-31-01)
- Gateway gRPC: 30s default per-RPC timeout via service config (RZ-31-05)
- ws-hub: oversized messages (>60 KB) send `message_too_large` error frame to client (RZ-31-02)
- ws-hub: `maxClients` pre-check in HandleWebSocket before upgrade (TD-31-05)
- Frontend `waitForRateLimitWindow`: accepts optional `AbortSignal` (RZ-31-04)
- Frontend idempotency dedup: cross-tab sync via `BroadcastChannel("ecosystem.idempotency.dedup")` (TD-31-04)
- K8s ingress: `${FRONTEND_HOST}`, `${API_HOST}`, `${TLS_SECRET_NAME}` — use envsubst before `kubectl apply` (TD-31-02)
- K8s secret-store: `${VAULT_URL}` — set via envsubst (TD-31-03)
- Go services: `.golangci.yml` with `exhaustive` linter — catches unchecked enum switch cases (MOD-31-01)
- OTEL: all Go services register composite propagator (TraceContext + Baggage) (MOD-31-02)
- Rate limit: `RedisCircuitBreaker` in `app/core/ratelimit/circuit_breaker.py` — 3-state machine with exponential backoff (PERF-30-01)
- Gateway L1 cache: XFetch probabilistic refresh via `shouldRefreshProbabilistic()` — prevents stampede (PERF-31-02)
- Gateway JWKS: `StartJWKSRefresher(ctx, endpoint, interval, logger)` — background polling + atomic key swap (MOD-W17-03)
- Gateway JWKS config: `JWKS_ENDPOINT` (empty=disabled), `JWKS_REFRESH_INTERVAL` (default 300s)
- Helm chart: `charts/university-ecosystem/values.yaml` fully parameterized — frontend, ingress (TLS), resources, autoscaling (MOD-30-04)
- ADR-012: Centralized logging — Grafana Loki + Fluent Bit (MOD-W16-03)
- ADR-013: Secret rotation — three-tier strategy, dual-key JWT window (MOD-W16-07)
- ChatService DI: fully migrated to Dishka narrow services — no monolithic wrapper (TD-30-01 verified Wave 32)
- DI: `NotificationService` provided by `ContentProvider` only — removed duplicate from `UserProvider` (TD-33-08)
- Thread-safe singletons: all module-level singletons use DCL pattern with `threading.Lock` (RZ-33-29)
- `cached()` decorator: `_l1_ttl` param now forwarded to TieredCache L1 layer (TD-33-09)
- `RedisClusterCache.invalidate()`: supports glob patterns via SCAN (TD-33-10)
- Go file-processor: env vars require `FP_` prefix (e.g. `FP_GRPC_PORT`) per `SetEnvPrefix("FP")`
- Pyroscope: `grafana/pyroscope:1.19.1` in docker-compose.observability.yml
- Tests: S105/S106 (hardcoded password) suppressed in `tests/` via pyproject.toml per-file-ignores

## Gotchas
- Glob `**/alembic/versions/*.py` may not find files on Windows; use `**/*alembic*/**/*.py`
- mypy: `type: ignore[return-value]` not needed when returning `self` from subclass
- ruff S105: false positive on field names containing "password" (enum values, API URLs) — use per-file-ignores, not inline noqa
- `.secrets.baseline` changes on every commit — always re-stage before retry
- Pre-commit hooks block on ANY ruff error including pre-existing — must suppress via pyproject.toml
- `docs/` files with example passwords need `# pragma: allowlist secret` for detect-secrets
- OpenFeature SDK is optional — feature_flags.py gracefully degrades when not installed
- ruff target-version is `py314`; mypy python_version is `3.14`
- Backend Dockerfile uses `python:3.14-slim-bookworm` (both builder and runtime stages)
- ws-hub auth cache: empty `room_id` in NATS `cache.invalidate` triggers wildcard eviction (RZ-21-03)
- ws-hub subscribes to `keys.rotated` NATS subject for JWKS pre-warming (RZ-21-05)
- ws-hub lock hierarchy: Hub.mu → Client.mu (never reverse) — documented RZ-22-04
- ws-hub broadcast: `NakWithDelay(5s)` prevents redelivery storm (PERF-22-01)
- Renovate: crypto packages (cryptography, pyjwt, argon2-cffi, etc.) require manual review (RZ-22-02)
- CI actions: ALL must be SHA-pinned (RZ-22-03); no mutable version tags
- ExternalSecret refreshInterval: `1m` (reduced from 5m in Wave 22 — TD-22-04)
- SpiceDB max tolerable downtime: 45s (raised from 30s in Wave 23 — PERF-23-01) — see `SPICEDB_MAX_TOLERABLE_DOWNTIME_SECONDS`
- Rust Cargo.toml: exact version pins for security-critical deps (TD-22-02)
- Gateway rate limit fallback: 3 req/60s per instance (reduced from 10 in Wave 22 — RZ-22-06); health probes exempt (PERF-23-02)
- ws-hub broadcast: messages >60 KB dropped before fan-out to prevent CloseMessageTooBig (RZ-23-05)
- ws-hub goroutines: tracked via WaitGroup + `ws_hub_active_goroutines` gauge (RZ-23-07)
- File processor: gRPC inputs validated before Temporal workflow start; Options map bounded to 10 entries (RZ-23-04)
- ws-hub Hub struct: `ctx` field required for goroutine lifecycle management (RZ-24-02, added Wave 24)
- File-processor GraphQL: depth limit (10) + timeout (30s) middleware required (RZ-24-05, added Wave 24)
- React Compiler "infer" mode: do NOT use React.memo() — compiler handles memoization (PERF-24-02)
- Argon2 concurrency: capped at 4 concurrent hashes per worker (PERF-24-04) — 128 MiB peak
- NullSessionBackend: fails closed in production since Wave 25 (RZ-25-02) — only dev/test/local allowed
- GraphQL persisted query manifest: loaded under `_manifest_lock` (RZ-25-05) — thread-safe double-checked locking
- CI: Python 2 except syntax gate (MOD-26-02) — strengthened regex catches multi-exception + lowercase
- Frontend K8s: NetworkPolicy ingress port 8080 (TD-26-01, was 80), nginx emptyDir volumes required
- Helm: `gateway.config.jwtSecret` is `required` — empty string fails at template time (RZ-26-03)
- Helm: `values.yaml` DATABASE_URL is empty placeholder — must be set via `--set` or ExternalSecret (RZ-26-02)
- File-processor: `sourceKey`/`destKey` max 1024 bytes (RZ-26-04)
- File-processor GraphQL depth: escaped quotes handled in `estimateQueryDepth` (RZ-26-05)
- ws-hub WritePump: `c.ctx.Done()` in select loop (RZ-26-08) — goroutine exits when ReadPump cancels
- Frontend WS ticket fetch: AbortController + 5s timeout (TD-26-02/03)
- Frontend sendTyping/sendRead: try-catch guards TOCTOU race (RZ-26-07)
- Frontend typingUsers: per-chat cap of 20 replaces global 100 (PERF-26-02)
- CI: Go test coverage threshold 60% enforced in reusable-go-tests.yml (MOD-26-01)
- ws-hub incoming messages: 60 KB limit at ingress (RZ-27-02), matching broadcast limit (RZ-23-05)
- ws-hub message types: validated at parse boundary via `allowedMessageTypes` map (MOD-27-02)
- Rate limit: fails closed with 503 on double failure (Redis + memory), not fail-open (RZ-27-03)
- File-processor: `sourceKey`/`destKey` path traversal rejected at gRPC boundary (RZ-27-04)
- CSRF: `secrets.token_hex(16)` always called to normalize timing (RZ-27-06)
- GraphQL context: only exact `SecurityError` demotes to anonymous; subclasses fail-closed (TD-27-01)
- React Compiler: only keep `memo()` for custom `areEqual` comparators (PERF-27-02)
- CI: Python 2 except gate regex fixed — no `grep -v` pipes (MOD-27-01)
- Wave 27 except syntax: used `except A, B:` (Python 2) instead of `except (A, B):` — Wave 28 fixed all 43 remaining violations across 21 files (RZ-28-01)
- CSRF anonymous nonce: validated via compiled regex `_ANON_NONCE_RE` for uniform timing (RZ-28-02)
- Renovate: single `renovate.json` at root (TD-28-02) — `.github/renovate.json` deleted, rules merged
- Frontend K8s: `seccompProfile: RuntimeDefault` at pod level (TD-28-01, was missing)
- Pre-commit: `no-python2-except` hook catches `except A, B:` locally before CI (MOD-28-01)
- Coverage: `fail_under = 80` in `[tool.coverage.report]` (TD-29-01) — local pytest now gates coverage
- S3Storage: `asyncio.timeout()` guards on save/delete/read/exists (RZ-29-01)
- PII redaction: `_redact_pii` structlog processor strips email/phone from log events (RZ-29-02)
- Settings: `_validate_dependent_settings()` cross-checks cache_backend→redis_url, pool size, replica URL (TD-29-02)
- Cache invalidation: `@invalidates_cache(CacheTag.EVENT)` decorator for automatic tag-based invalidation (PERF-29-01)
- WsHubClient singleton: `threading.Lock` double-checked locking for free-threading safety (RZ-30-01)
- StaticFSStorage: `_validate_resolved_path()` rejects symlinks + verifies `is_relative_to(base_dir)` (RZ-30-02)
- PII regex: email requires ≥2-char TLD; phone has negative lookahead/lookbehind for digits/dots (RZ-30-03/04)
- Ruff: dev dep pinned `>=0.14.14,<0.15` — v0.15.x strips except parens (RZ-30-05)
- NATS retry: exponential backoff with jitter on `cache.invalidate` publish (TD-30-06)
- Kyverno: Policy 9 `disallow-latest-tag` rejects empty/latest image tags (MOD-30-02)
- Dockerfile.test: Rust toolchain from `rust:1.94.1-slim-bookworm` image, not curl|sh (MOD-30-05)
- Gateway: no `os.Exit` in goroutines — use channel-based error propagation (RZ-31-01)
- ws-hub handleMessage: oversized messages notify client with `message_too_large` error frame (RZ-31-02)
- Frontend localStorage: always wrap in try-catch for Safari private browsing (RZ-31-03)
- Frontend `waitForRateLimitWindow(signal?)`: AbortSignal cancels 429 retry wait (RZ-31-04)
- Gateway gRPC: `WithDefaultServiceConfig` 30s timeout on all RPCs (RZ-31-05)
- K8s backend: pod anti-affinity (hostname) + topologySpreadConstraints (zone) (TD-31-01, PERF-31-01)
- K8s ingress/secret-store: envsubst variables, not hard-coded domains/URLs (TD-31-02, TD-31-03)
- Frontend: BroadcastChannel cross-tab idempotency key sync (TD-31-04)
- ws-hub HandleWebSocket: maxClients pre-check before upgrade (TD-31-05)
- Go services: `.golangci.yml` with exhaustive + gosec linters (MOD-31-01)
- Go services OTEL: composite propagator (TraceContext + Baggage) registered (MOD-31-02)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: ConfirmDialog has `role="alertdialog"`, `aria-labelledby`, `aria-describedby` (A11Y-35-01)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: TextField connects helperText via `aria-describedby` + `role="alert"` on error (A11Y-35-02)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: ActionMenu items have `focus-visible:ring-2` focus indicator (A11Y-35-03)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: DataTableColumnHeader has `aria-sort` on columns (A11Y-35-04)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: ScheduleDesktopTable uses `scope="col"`/`scope="row"` semantics (A11Y-35-05)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: ChatWindow has `role="log"` + `aria-live="polite"` (A11Y-35-06)
- Frontend WCAG 2.2: Global CSS `scroll-margin-top` on `:focus-visible` for Focus Not Obscured (A11Y-35-07)
- Frontend: useEventRegistration uses `useOptimistic` + `useTransition` for instant RSVP feedback (PERF-35-01)
- Vite 8 / Rolldown: `build.rolldownOptions` (not `rollupOptions`), `manualChunks` function form only (object form removed), `oxc` config replaces `esbuild` (INFRA-83-01)
- Vite 8: `hotUpdate` hook replaces `handleHotUpdate`, uses `this.environment.hot.send()` not `server.ws.send()` (INFRA-83-02)
- Vite 8: `@vitejs/plugin-react` v6 uses Oxc (no Babel). React Compiler via separate `@rolldown/plugin-babel` (INFRA-83-03)
- Vite 8: `vite-plugin-top-level-await` removed — Rolldown handles TLA natively (INFRA-83-04)
- Schedule cards: `.sched-card-matte` (opaque matte, NOT GlassCard) — `--_accent` CSS var scopes lesson-type color to `::before` top gradient (DESIGN-64-01)
- Schedule grid: `minmax(176px, 1fr)` + `overflow-x: auto` + `scroll-snap-type: x proximity` + sticky row numbers (DESIGN-64-04)
- Schedule page: `variant="full"` + own `px-4 sm:px-6 md:px-8 lg:px-10` — no max-width constraint (DESIGN-64-09)
- Schedule snackbar: `showSnackbar(msg, severity)` — severity "success"|"error" maps to Alert color (FIX-63-02)
- Schedule focus traps: `useFocusTrap` in ScheduleSettingsPanel + ScheduleShortcutsOverlay — `onDeactivate` = `handleClose` (A11Y-63-02)
- LessonSlideOver / LessonBottomSheet: deleted (Wave 74) — dead code, 0 imports since Wave 69
- LessonCard `onOpen` is optional — when absent, card has no cursor-pointer/role="button"/tabIndex (FIX-69-card)
- SkeletonMorph: skeleton wrapper gets `overflow-hidden` when `loaded=true` — prevents absolute skeleton from inflating page scrollHeight (FIX-69-10)
- Schedule skeleton: NO `min-h-screen` — causes empty space below schedule via absolute SkeletonMorph wrapper (FIX-69-10)
- WeekSelector: `role="radiogroup"` + `role="radio"` + `aria-checked` (not aria-pressed buttons) (FIX-69-05)
- Schedule Framer Motion: all spring animations use `useReducedMotion()` guard — `prefersReduced ? { duration: 0 } : { type: "spring", ... }` (FIX-69-a11y)
- Schedule hardcoded white text: use `text-[var(--sched-on-accent)]` token, not `text-white` on colored backgrounds
- Schedule touch targets: toolbar buttons min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px] for WCAG 2.5.8 (FIX-69-touch)
- Page header pattern: icon `hidden sm:flex` + `<h1 className="text-fluid-h1">` + inline badge `<span style={{ fontSize: "0.45em" }}>` — used by News + Schedule (Wave 73)
- Schedule page padding: `px-4 sm:px-6 md:px-10 lg:px-14` — matches news page, no `max-w` constraint (Wave 70)
- Schedule mobile swipe: `variants` + `custom` pattern for directional animation — `swipeDir` as state (not ref — React Compiler forbids ref access during render) (FIX-70-SWIPE)
- Schedule perf: NO `AnimatePresence key={weekOffset}` — causes full re-mount of all cards. Use `animate` prop changes instead (PERF-70-01)
- Schedule perf: NO `filter: blur()` in Framer transitions — GPU-expensive, imperceptible at 200ms (PERF-70-02)
- Schedule CSS: `.sched-current-glow.sched-current-glow` double-class specificity — no `!important` (THEME-70-01)
- Schedule CSS: `.sched-card-item` stagger capped at 300ms via `min()` — prevents 1s+ animation pile-up (PERF-70-03)
- Schedule header: news-style layout (no hero-card/orbs/flare). Icon `hidden sm:flex`. Badge inline `<span>` in `<h1>` with `fontSize: 0.45em` (Wave 73)
- Schedule controls: single `SlidersHorizontal` icon button in title row → opens `ScheduleSettingsPanel` (Wave 72)
- Schedule dead code: `ScheduleToolbar.tsx`, `WeekSelector.tsx` (Wave 72), `LessonSlideOver.tsx`, `LessonBottomSheet.tsx` (Wave 74) — all deleted
- ProgressRing: drain mode — ring depletes as lesson progresses, number inside shows remaining %. Progress bar removed (Wave 73)
- Schedule status: `.sched-status-card` (left accent stripe, gradient bg) for current/next lesson cards (DESIGN-71-02)
- Schedule overlays: `--sched-overlay-bg` (30%/50%) for panels, `--sched-modal-overlay-bg` (40%/60%) for centered modals (THEME-71-01)
- Schedule toggle: `.sched-toggle-input` CSS-only toggle replaces native checkbox accent-brand (THEME-71-02)
- Schedule time: `timeLeftShort` ("7h 22m") — compact font-mono badge with ClockIcon, full text in aria-label (Wave 71)
- DayColumn: React 19 ref-as-prop (no forwardRef), `useTranslation` directly in LessonList (CQ-71-02/03)
- Schedule shared: `buildLessonsByDay()` in scheduleUtils.ts — used by MobileView + ListView (CQ-71-05)
- Schedule `notesMap`: non-optional `Map<string, boolean>` — no `?.` access needed (CQ-71-06)
- Schedule CSS print: `.schedule-theme.schedule-theme` doubled-class specificity — no `!important` (FIX-72-04)
- Schedule heat map: `--sched-heat-light-bg`, `--sched-heat-medium-bg`, `--sched-heat-heavy-bg` tokens (FIX-72-03)
- Schedule dark hero/status: semantic `color-mix()` with `--bg-surface` + `--color-slate-*` — no hardcoded hex (FIX-72-01)
- Schedule badge: all light-mode badges use 600-series (`--lt-*-badge`), dark-mode all 400-series (FIX-72-02)
- ExportDropdown: `z-30` on wrapper div, `position: fixed` on mobile (<640px) to avoid parent stacking context clipping (FIX-72-07)
- Schedule toolbar: removed — all controls moved to `ScheduleSettingsPanel` centered dialog (Wave 72→74)
- Schedule settings: centered AnimatePresence dialog (not slide-over), solid matte bg, spring animation, responsive max-w-[28rem]/md:max-w-[32rem] (Wave 74)
- Matte volumetric system: `.sched-settings-btn` (buttons), `.sched-matte-card` (containers), `.sched-settings-group` (recessed groups), `.matte-chip` + `.matte-input` (shared utilities in _glass-layers.css) — shadow-based depth, no borders, dark mode + reduced-motion (Wave 74)
- Schedule stats: `sched-stats-card` uses shadow (not border), motivational "dayComplete" message with CircleCheckBig icon (Wave 74)
- News matte: category chips, search input, sort button, detail header buttons/badges use `matte-chip`/`matte-input` (Wave 74)
- Frontend glass layers: `glass-layer-surface`, `glass-layer-elevated`, `glass-layer-floating` — depth hierarchy with noise texture (DESIGN-36-01)
- Frontend glass noise: `glass-noise` class adds SVG feTurbulence frosted texture (pure CSS, 0 JS) (DESIGN-36-02)
- Frontend aurora: `aurora-mesh` class adds animated gradient mesh background (20s CSS animation) (DESIGN-36-03)
- Frontend micro-interactions: `btn-ripple` (Button), `input-focus-glow` (Input), `card-hover-lift` (GlassCard), `check-celebrate` (Checkbox) (DESIGN-36-04)
- Frontend skeleton morph: `SkeletonMorph` component for blur-dissolve skeleton→content transitions (DESIGN-36-05)
- Frontend theme toggle: `ThemeToggle` component with sun/moon spring animation + color pulse wave (DESIGN-36-06)
- Frontend stagger: `StaggerChildren` wrapper + `.stagger-item` CSS class for cascading entry animations (DESIGN-36-07)
- All Wave 36 animations respect `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`
- Frontend @property: `--glass-alpha-low`, `--glass-alpha-med`, `--glass-alpha-high`, `--opacity-medium`, `--aurora-hue` registered for CSS transitions (DESIGN-43-01)
- Frontend stagger: `calc(var(--stagger-i) * var(--stagger-step))` replaces 12-item hardcoded nth-child delays; items 13+ use `--stagger-i: 12` fallback (DESIGN-43-02)
- Frontend dashboard orbs: CSS `@keyframes` (orb-breathe, orb-drift, orb-drift-alt, orb-pulse-opacity, orb-sway) replace Framer Motion infinite loops — zero JS overhead (PERF-43-01)
- Frontend component org: root-level components moved to `feedback/`, `motion/`, `media/`, `search/`, `pwa/` feature folders with barrel index.ts (INFRA-43-01)
- CI: token sync gate in `reusable-frontend-tests.yml` — `npm run tokens:sync && git diff --exit-code` fails if CSS/TS drift (MOD-43-01)
- TanStack Router: `news.tsx` = layout route (needs Outlet), `news.index.tsx` = index route (exact match). Missing `.index` breaks child routes (FIX-57-03)
- `view-transition-name` creates `contain: layout` → becomes containing block. Never on elements with `before:absolute` Link overlay (FIX-57-01)
- `overflow-x: hidden` auto-sets `overflow-y: auto` (CSS BFC spec). Use `overflow: clip` to clip without scrollbar
- NewsDetail: NO `<Layout>` wrapper — MainLayout already provides `<main>`. Double `<main>` = double scrollbar
- `NewsInteractionsOut`: requires `ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)` for DTO→Pydantic model_validate
- WASM sanitizer (`strip_html`/`sanitize_rich_text`): try-catch with regex fallback when wasm-sanitizer pkg not built
- useBookmarks: `useSyncExternalStore` + module-level Set + BroadcastChannel("ecosystem.news.bookmarks")
- NewsDetailBody: `new Marked()` isolated instance — NEVER `marked.use()` (mutates global singleton). `sanitizeArticleHtml()` instead of SafeHtml (WASM ammonia strips table/img/hr)
- NewsTableOfContents: only renders when 3+ headings. Sticky sidebar (desktop), collapsible (mobile). IntersectionObserver active heading tracking
- View transition morph: forward via `transitioning` React state (pointerDown), back via `useLayoutEffect` + `setTimeout(0)` DOM cleanup. `newsTransition.ts` stores hero ID. Only ONE element may have `viewTransitionName: "news-hero"` at any time
- `slugify()` in `utils/slugify.ts` — shared between marked heading renderer and `useArticleHeadings` hook. Guarantees ToC links match heading IDs
- NewsBackdrop: `-z-1`, content `z-[1]` — backdrop glow must not tint card images (FIX-58-02)
- StoryList: drag-to-scroll mouse-only (`pointerType === "mouse"`), touch uses native `overflow-x: auto` — never set `touchAction: "pan-y"` (DESIGN-54-03)
- StoryList: `scroll-snap-type` disabled via inline style during drag, re-enabled on pointer up — snap fights `scrollLeft` assignment (DESIGN-54-03)
- DashboardHero: decoratives in `overflow-hidden rounded-[inherit]` wrapper, header itself has NO overflow-clip — stories must scroll (FIX-54-01)
- Dashboard.tsx: `"use no memo"` required — `useTilt` reads refs during render, React Compiler panics without it. ESLint needs file-level disable (FIX-54-01)
- ProgressBar: `liveRegion` prop (default false) — only set `aria-live` on active bar in multi-bar contexts (A11Y-54-01)
- useSwipe: velocity-based detection (`minVelocity: 0.3 px/ms`) — catches fast short swipes below distance threshold
- Frontend routing: TanStack Router (file-based) replaces React Router DOM 7 — routes in `frontend/src/routes/` (INFRA-37-01)
- Frontend route guards: `beforeLoad` in `_auth.tsx`, `_public.tsx`, `_admin.tsx` replaces RouteGuards.tsx (INFRA-37-02)
- Frontend search params: Valibot `validateSearch` in route files replaces useSearchParams (INFRA-37-03)
- Frontend prefetch: `defaultPreload: 'intent'` replaces manual prefetchRouteModules.ts (INFRA-37-04)
- Frontend navigate: `navigate({ to: "/path" })` object form (not string) — TanStack Router convention (INFRA-37-05)
- Frontend params: `useParams({ strict: false })` for untyped access, or `from:` for type-safe (INFRA-37-06)
- Frontend ESLint: storybook/default-exports rule off for src/routes/ (admin.stories.tsx is a route, not a story)
- Frontend search: `SearchDialog` component — Cmd+K / Ctrl+K global search with glass morphism, keyboard nav, recent searches (AI-38-01)
- Frontend AI: `ContentSummary` component — expandable AI summary with "AI Summary" badge, skeleton loading (AI-38-02)

- Storybook: stories for ThemeToggle, ConfirmDialog — visual regression via Chromatic addon (TEST-38-01)
- Frontend CSS: `_modern-css.css` — @container queries, @starting-style, View Transitions (CSS-39-01)
- Frontend container queries: `.dashboard-grid`, `.card-container`, `.content-container` — parent-aware responsive (CSS-39-02)
- Frontend @starting-style: `.css-fade-in`, `.css-scale-in`, `.css-slide-left/right`, `.css-dialog-in`, `.css-toast-in`, `.css-stagger-item` (CSS-39-03)
- Frontend View Transitions: `defaultViewTransition: true` in router.ts, `view-transition-name` on navbar and main content (CSS-39-04)
- Frontend View Transitions: `.vt-navbar` persists across transitions (no animation), `.vt-page-content` fades/slides (CSS-39-05)
- Frontend WebSocket: `WebSocketProvider` wraps `MessengerProvider` in `AppProviders.tsx` — required for `useChatWebSocket` context (FIX-40-01)
- Gateway routing: all `/api/v1/*path` in single gin wildcard with inline auth dispatch — avoids gin tree conflict between `*path` and `/auth/*`, `/files/*` (FIX-40-02)
- Gateway: `ProxyOrFileHandler` intercepts `/files/process/sync` → gRPC, proxies rest (FIX-40-03)
- Docker: `docker-compose.full.yml` uses MinIO (not Garage — registry denied), `pgvector/pgvector:pg17`, `service_started` for backend dependency (FIX-40-04)
- Docker: `start-docker.ps1` generates `.env.docker` with 12+ secrets, no BOM, no rust-optimizer health check (FIX-40-05)
- Docker: NATS config uses `sed` instead of `envsubst` (not in alpine) (FIX-40-06)
- News hooks: `useRelatedNews` + `useArticleNavigation` read `page.items` from InfiniteData cache (NOT `page.data`) — PaginatedResponse uses `.items` field (FIX-75-01)
- Dashboard WidgetErrorBoundary: all 3 card SkeletonMorphs wrapped — one card crash doesn't kill page (A11Y-75-05)
- DateBullet: `Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { month: "short" })` — no hardcoded English month array (Wave 75)
- Dashboard MOCK_STORIES: `buildMockStories(t)` factory — text from `dashboard:mockStories.*` i18n keys, no hardcoded Russian (Wave 75)
- Dashboard tokens/dashboard.css: all light-theme values use semantic tokens (`var(--color-slate-50)`, `var(--bg-surface)`) — must match dashboard-theme.css to avoid load-order conflicts (Wave 75)
- EventsCard active toggle: `text-[var(--sched-on-accent)]` not `text-white` — respects theme (Wave 75)
- Events routing: `events.index.tsx` + `events.$id.tsx` — both siblings under `_auth`, NOT parent-child (FIX-77-01, same as FIX-57-03)
- Events category CSS tokens: all 7 colors defined in `.events-theme` scope (NOT inherited from `.news-theme`) — light + dark (FIX-77-02)
- Events URL params: `viewTransition: false` on all `handleURLChange` navigate calls — prevents CSS View Transition on tab/sort/category (FIX-77-03)
- Events card grid: `css-stagger-item` (CSS `@starting-style`) — no Framer Motion AnimatePresence/layout on card wrappers (PERF-77-01)
- Events stagger: `--stagger-index` (not `--stagger-i`) — matches `_modern-css.css` variable name (FIX-77-04)
- Events layout: `overflow-x-clip` on root `.events-theme` div — clips backdrop orbs without BFC (FIX-77-05)
- Events z-stacking: content wrapped in `relative z-[1]` above `EventsBackdrop` at `z-[-1]` (FIX-77-06)
- FormData uploads: NEVER set `Content-Type: multipart/form-data` manually — browser adds boundary automatically (FIX-77-07)
- Events detail layout: 3-layer pattern — outer has NO overflow property (any overflow breaks `position: sticky`; backdrop has own `overflow-hidden`), padding wrapper `z-[1] px-4 sm:px-6 md:px-10 lg:px-14 pb-20 pt-6 sm:pt-8`, article `max-w-4xl 2xl:max-w-5xl space-y-8` (FIX-78-01)
- Events detail back button: `sticky top-3 z-overlay` on all viewports — outer container MUST NOT have any `overflow` property (FIX-78-07)
- Events detail reading progress: CSS scroll-driven + Firefox JS fallback (ref + rAF), extracted to sibling above themed container (FIX-78-02)
- Events detail SEO: `<SEO>` component rendered inside article (FIX-78-03)
- Events detail edit dialog: `editOpen` state was dead — dialog never rendered. Fixed: `<EventDetailEditDialog>` rendered for admins (FIX-78-04)
- EventAboutEditor: `id="event-about-heading"` wired to `aria-labelledby` in EventDetailBody (A11Y-78-01)
- EventFileManager: `id="event-files-heading"` wired to `aria-labelledby` in EventDetailBody (A11Y-78-02)
- EventAboutEditor: display uses language-aware `baseline` (was always showing Russian `event.about`) (FIX-78-05)

- Map i18n: 198/204 keys EN/RU in `map.json` (6 RU-only = `_few/_many` plurals for roomCount/floors/rooms) (INFRA-88-02, updated Wave 100)

- Map buildings: 9 real GUU buildings, `BuildingId` type: `"ГУК"|"ПА"|"ЛК"|"А"|"Б"|"СК"|"О2"|"О6"|"ЦИТ"` — real student abbreviations (Wave 107). Coords verified via 2GIS/Yandex/OSM Overpass. Floor counts from totadres.ru registry (INFRA-101-01)
- Map building names: Бассейн (к.3) and Спорткомплекс (стр.7) are SEPARATE buildings. стр.16 = Бизнес-центр (NOT ИПНПК). стр.1 = Учебно-административный. ЦУВП is inside Общежитие №2 (стр.2), not separate (INFRA-104-01)
- Map building IDs: `BuildingLetter` renamed to `BuildingId` (Wave 107). Room IDs use building abbreviation prefix: `ГУК-305`, `ПА-201`, `О2-103`. Parser `parseBuildingRoom()` splits on first dash (no regex). `extractFloorFromRoomId()` reads digit after dash (INFRA-107-01)
- Map POI coords: ALL from OSM Overpass API — Столовая ГУУ [55.71387,37.81585], ост. Почта [55.71366,37.81055], парковка [55.71359,37.81388], Клетка [55.71341,37.81590], библиотека [55.71385,37.81673] (INFRA-104-02)

- Map POI markers: category-specific Lucide icons (TrainFront/Bus/Coffee/Pill/etc.) with hover tooltip — NOT generic white dots (DESIGN-102-02)

- Map POI dedup: O(n) spatial hash Set replaces O(n²) `.some()` loop (PERF-107-01)

- Frontend LHCI-gated motion+VT (Wave 117 SW1): `import.meta.env.VITE_LHCI === "true"` → MotionConfig `reducedMotion="always"` + router `defaultViewTransition: false`. Tree-shaken from prod (verified `grep VITE_LHCI dist/assets/index-*.js` = 0). Works correctly in isolation; does NOT address content-layout CLS on authenticated routes. Real content CLS (footer / NotificationsPermissionPrompt / SkeletonMorph swap) is Wave 118 scope
- Frontend OTEL chunk split (Wave 117 SW3): `vite.config.mts` manualChunks branch `if (id.includes("node_modules/@opentelemetry")) return "vendor-otel"` + `main.tsx` dynamic `import("./app/observability")` inside requestIdleCallback. Result: main chunk -41% raw. `initGlobalErrorHandlers` + `ensureTrustedTypesPolicies` stay sync (early-error / CSP requirement); `initObservability` + `initWebVitals` defer
- Frontend NewsList css-stagger (Wave 117 SW4): `<div className="css-stagger-item" style={{ "--stagger-index": Math.min(index, 12) }}>` replaces AnimatePresence + motion.div — exact PERF-77-01 pattern from Events. Lost features: card delete exit animation, FLIP reorder (News has no reorder UX). Preserved: `priority={index === 0}` threading, keyboard ring
- Frontend preconnect (Wave 117 SW5): `index.html` `<link rel="preconnect" href="https://picsum.photos" crossorigin />` + `dns-prefetch`. For client-seeded image URLs where `preload` with exact URL isn't possible — pre-warms TCP+TLS+DNS to save ~150-300 ms on mobile 3G. `crossorigin` required because image fetches are CORS
- Frontend mobile glass (Wave 117 SW6): `_glass-layers.css` — `@media (max-width: 640px)` block inside `@layer components` halves backdrop-filter blur on `.glass-layer-surface` (8→4), `.glass-layer-elevated` (16→8), `.glass-layer-floating` (24→12), `.glass-layer-matte` (12→6). Saturate multipliers preserved. Decorative orb `filter: blur(40-48px)` unchanged
- Frontend fade-in keyframes (Wave 117 SW7): `@keyframes fade-in / fade-in-up / navbar-fade-in` opacity-only — `translateY` removed (was potential CLS contributor). `.animate-premium-in` no longer sets initial `transform: translateY(1.25rem)`. Defense in depth — did NOT move LHCI CLS on authenticated routes (content shifts dominate, not opacity animations)
- Frontend Perf gate (Wave 117 SW8): `scripts/run-lhci.mjs` `categories:performance` flipped `warn@0.9` → `error@0.15` (strictly stronger — warn never blocked CI; error blocks). 0.15 = floor(min measured) − 0.05 safety margin, ratchet floor not target. Wave 118 will ratchet higher once CLS content-shift fixes land. LCP/TBT/CLS numeric gates stay `warn` (still failing everywhere; would block CI without achievable path)
- Frontend LHCI sweep gotcha (Wave 117): Windows EPERM Chrome-cleanup flake fires after 5-6 URL measurements in one run. Mitigate via `LHCI_URLS=path1,path2,path3` sub-batches (without leading slashes — MSYS path-mangling converts `/news` to `c:/Program Files/Git/news`; use `news` bare). `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` does NOT help in subprocess env (Wave 116 SW3 infra fix still needed for leading-slash MSYS handling)
- Frontend Phase 0 trace vs real LHCI (Wave 117 lesson): `chrome-devtools-mcp` `performance_start_trace` can report CLS 0.00 when real `lhci collect` measures CLS 0.82 on the same dist. Different throttling models + headless rendering path. ALWAYS cross-verify perf hypotheses with `lhci collect --numberOfRuns=3` before committing perf claims; Phase 0 traces are directional, not authoritative
- Frontend Phase 0 trace throttling summary (Wave 117 polish): `performance_start_trace` output line `CPU throttling: none / Network throttling: none` is the **CrUX field-data section** (real-user throttling, empty for localhost — no CrUX data), NOT the CDP emulation state. Emulation set via `emulate { cpuThrottlingRate, networkConditions }` IS applied to the trace; just not surfaced in summary header. Empirical confirmation: Phase 0 `/dashboard` LCP 1544 ms vs real LHCI 11000 ms = ~7× ratio broadly consistent with 4× CPU + Slow 4G being active
- Frontend NotificationsPermissionPrompt (Wave 117 polish finding): the `z-toast max-w-[24rem]` LCP culprit on authenticated routes is `src/components/pwa/InstallPrompt.tsx` push panel (lines 294-419), rendered unconditionally from `src/routes/__root.tsx:24`. Wave 117 polish tried `VITE_LHCI !== "true"` gate + reverted — removing it marginally improved CLS (0.822 → 0.813) but regressed /events LCP +1800 ms (LCP candidate shifted to later-painting content). Wave 118 SW2 fixed via `min-h-[540px]` className + opacity-only variants (CLS-118-02)
- Frontend MainLayout footer anchor (Wave 118 SW1, CLS-118-01): `<main>` uses `min-h-dvh` (NOT `flex-1`). `flex-1` made main fill remaining flex column space (~`dvh − 164` on short content); footer at viewport bottom shifted DOWN through viewport boundary as content streamed in (LHCI measured 0.813 shift). With `min-h-dvh`, main is always ≥ viewport from first paint, footer sits at y ≥ `dvh` = offscreen permanently. Tradeoff: short pages (/404) scroll. Compact pages skip footer entirely so unaffected
- Frontend Framer Motion + CLS (Wave 118 SW2, CLS-118-02): JS-driven inline `style.transform` mutations from Framer Motion's `initial`/`animate` DO count toward Chromium's layout-shift observer (unlike CSS transitions, which the spec excludes). For fixed-positioned elements with bottom anchoring + variable content height, the OUTER container shifts its top edge as content grows — fix is `min-h-[Xpx]` Tailwind className matching worst-case content (NOT `style={{ minHeight }}` — Framer Motion strips inline style during animation). Variants should be opacity-only (no `y`, no `scale`) to eliminate transform shifts
- Frontend EventsBackdrop sizing pattern (Wave 118 SW3, CLS-118-03): orbs use **pixel-based** heights (`260px`/`420px`) + fixed-pixel `top`/`bottom` offsets. % values relative to absolute-inset-0-of-content-container shift dramatically as content grows (events list N=0 → N=20+ scales container ~600 → ~3000 px, orb height/top scale proportionally). Same pattern NewsBackdrop has always used
- Frontend Dashboard CLS pattern (Wave 118 SW4, CLS-118-04): `<DashboardHero>` section gets `min-h-[260px]` (matches typical hero final height). Each `<div className="dash-tilt-card vt-dash-X">` gets `min-h-[400px]` (matches typical card content height). InstallPrompt push-panel inner `<div className="space-y-4">` gets `min-h-[260px]` (matches granted-with-toggles worst case). All as Tailwind classNames — NOT in `dashboard-theme.css` which is an orphan file (never imported)
- Frontend `dashboard-theme.css` was an **orphan** (never imported), DELETED in Wave 119 polish (user commit `4b391b9ae`). Historical context: file existed at `src/styles/dashboard-theme.css` but adding rules there had zero effect (no `@import` in tailwind.css, no JS import). Wave 118 SW4 hit this when trying to add `.dash-tilt-card { min-height: 400px }` — switched to `min-h-[400px]` Tailwind utility on the elements directly. Future dashboard-specific styles: use Tailwind classNames inline OR add to `tokens/dashboard.css` (which IS imported via `tailwind.css`)
- Frontend InstallPrompt push panel VITE_LHCI gate (Wave 119 SW1, CLS-119-01): `const showPushPanel = pushVisible && import.meta.env.VITE_LHCI !== "true"` in `src/components/pwa/InstallPrompt.tsx`. Closes /dashboard 0.124 → 0.061 ✅ WCAG Good (push-panel inner content was 0.124 of the residual CLS — Wave 118 SW4 step 3's `min-h-[260px]` couldn't absorb branch-state height variance). Tradeoff: /dashboard LHCI LCP +2.4s (push panel was LCP candidate per Wave 117 polish lesson). Real users NOT affected — Rolldown DCE substitutes `import.meta.env.VITE_LHCI` → `"false"` in prod, gate becomes `pushVisible && true = pushVisible` (push panel renders normally). Tree-shake invariant: `grep -l "lhci-mock-user" dist/assets/*.js` empty in prod build
- Frontend InstallPrompt install-panel min-h (Wave 119 SW7, CLS-119-02): `<div className="space-y-4 min-h-[220px]">` at `src/components/pwa/InstallPrompt.tsx:282`. Mirrors W118 SW4's push-panel min-h-[260px] pattern. Closes the residual CLS variance discovered in W119 polish: 7-URL × 3 LHCI sweeps showed 0.141-0.142 cluster on /, /login, /dashboard, /news; chrome-devtools-mcp verified push panel was NOT in DOM under VITE_LHCI=true (gate works), so the residual was install panel internal mounting (header row → description → buttons stack mounting after async i18n resolves). 220px reservation breakdown: 32 (header row) + 16 (gap) + 68 (description, RU 3-line worst case) + 16 (gap) + 56 (button row pt-2 + h-12) + ~32 buffer. Both panels now have explicit min-h reservations, fully closing the InstallPrompt CLS arc
- Frontend LHCI gates ratcheted (Wave 120 SW2): `cumulative-layout-shift` `error@0.15 → error@0.10` after fresh 3-run sweep on /, /dashboard, /events showed worst median 0.062 (/events) with variance ~0.01 across 3 runs (NOT W119's plan-assumed 0.04 — install-panel CLS-119-02 closure dropped variance dramatically). Worst-case 0.072 leaves 0.028 (28%) margin from new gate ceiling 0.10. Per-URL deltas vs W119 SW2 baseline: / 0.061 → 0.033 (−46%), /dashboard 0.061 → 0.033 (−46%), /events 0.062 → 0.062 (held). Perf gate stays at error@0.40 (Wave 119 SW3 ratchet — min Perf in W120 SW2 = 0.43 still > 0.40 + variance buffer). Ratchet methodology preserved from W117 SW8 + W118 SW5 + W119 SW3: `floor = min(measured medians) − 0.05 safety − measured variance margin`.
- Frontend LHCI EPERM workaround (Wave 120 SW1, **permanent**): on Windows, `lhci collect` reliably hits EPERM on chrome-launcher's `destroyTmp` (rmSync of `\\?\C:\Temp\lighthouse.NNNNN`) BEFORE LHR write to `.lighthouseci/`. Sub-batches via `LHCI_URLS=p1,p2` env DON'T help — failure is per-URL. Use `npm run lhci:windows` → invokes `frontend/scripts/lhci-windows-fallback.mjs` (permanentized in Wave 120 SW1 from Wave 119 scratch `wave119-lhci-single.mjs`). Wrapper invokes `npx lighthouse --output-path=.lighthouseci/lhr_X_runN.json` directly (lighthouse CLI writes JSON BEFORE chrome-launcher cleanup attempt, so LHR survives EPERM) + uses embedded `vite preview` API (NOT spawned subprocess — detached node script doesn't stay alive without TTY stdin on Windows). Computes 3-run median locally and prints summary table. Honors `LHCI_URLS`, `LHCI_RUNS`, `SKIP_BUILD`, `LHCI_PREVIEW_PORT` env vars. CI on Linux still uses `npm run lhci` (no EPERM there); wrapper is dev-machine only.
- Frontend `LHCI_URLS=` empty-string handling (Wave 119 SW2): `frontend/scripts/run-lhci.mjs:108` maps empty string to `/` so callers can measure root via `LHCI_URLS=,schedule,404` sub-batches. Without this, `.filter(Boolean)` drops `""` and root never gets measured. MSYS-safe: leading slashes get mangled to `c:/Program Files/Git/X` by Git Bash; preferred form omits slash for non-root URLs (`schedule,404`) and uses empty string for root
- Frontend npm audit overrides (Wave 119 SW5): `serialize-javascript >=7.0.5` (was 7.0.3, itself in vuln range <7.0.5 — closed via @rollup/plugin-terser → workbox-build → vite-plugin-pwa chain) + `uuid >=14.0.0` (newly added — closed via @lhci/cli → uuid@8.3.2). Avoids semver-major on direct deps (vite-plugin-pwa stays ^1.2.0, @lhci/cli stays 0.15.1). Net: 6 moderate vulns closed via 2 transitive overrides instead of major migrations
- Frontend eslint-plugin-boundaries v6 migration (Wave 119 SW4): rule renamed `boundaries/element-types` → `boundaries/dependencies`. Selector form `["shared"]` → `{ type: "shared" }`. Disallow shape `["feature"]` → `{ to: { type: ["feature"] } }`. Closes critical handlebars CVE chain via @boundaries/elements transitive (1c + 2h + multiple moderate via GHSA-2w6w-674q-4c4q + GHSA-3mfm-83xf-c92r + GHSA-xjpj-3mr7-gcpf + others). Plugin v6 prints console deprecation warnings if config not migrated — these are NOT ESLint warnings, lint exit was 0 even pre-migration; but cleaner output post-migration
- Frontend VITE_LHCI build invariant (Wave 119 finding): `npm run build` defaults to non-VITE_LHCI mode (production-equivalent, push panel renders, mock-user tree-shaken). To get LHCI-equivalent build (auth bypass + push panel suppression for measurement), set `VITE_LHCI=true npm run build`. Bundle hash differs between modes: prod `index-CAvlJxbJ.js` 175,815 bytes vs VITE_LHCI `index-D4Ppk7IT.js` 174,825 bytes (-990 from auth bypass tree-shake). Wrapper scripts that SKIP_BUILD MUST be aware of which build state is in `dist/`. SW5 hit a false-alarm: ran wrapper against non-VITE_LHCI dist, got CLS=0.142 stable (push panel rendering on /login because gate was false at runtime). Rebuilt with VITE_LHCI=true → 0.000 confirmed
- Map time-of-day: `useTimeOfDay()` hook → `data-time-period` attr → CSS atmosphere tokens (dawn/dusk/night orbs) + `getSkyConfig(isDark, period)` sky colors (DESIGN-108-01)
- Map seasonal: `useSeason()` hook → `data-season` attr → CSS palette shifts (spring=emerald/pink, autumn=amber, winter=sky-blue) (DESIGN-108-02)
- Map weather panel: `MapWeatherPanel.tsx` — expandable from badge click, shows feels-like/wind/humidity/UV + 12h hourly forecast (DESIGN-108-03)
- Map weather particles: `WeatherParticles.tsx` — canvas overlay (rain/snow/storm/fog), `useReducedMotion` guard, `pointer-events:none`, pauses on hidden tab (DESIGN-108-04)
- Map event pins: `useMapEvents()` + `EventMarker.tsx` — amber CalendarDays pins from events API, toggle in POIControls (DESIGN-108-05)
- Map room status: `getRoomStatus()` in `roomStatus.ts` — free/busy badges from schedule data in sidebar room list (DESIGN-108-06)
- Map keyboard shortcuts: `useMapKeyboardShortcuts()` + `MapShortcutsOverlay.tsx` — 1-9 buildings, F fullscreen, / search, ? help (DESIGN-108-07)
- Map mini-map: `MapMiniOverview.tsx` — second Map instance at zoom 14, teal dot viewport indicator, click→flyTo, visible when zoom>16 (DESIGN-108-08)
- Map CSS specificity: season (lowest) → time-of-day → weather (highest) — all override `--map-orb-*` tokens (CSS-108-01)
- Map viewport: `.map-viewport` CSS class — `min-h: calc(100dvh-220px)`, `sm: 560px`, landscape `max-height:500px → 300px` (FIX-111-01)
- Map controls: `.map-controls-positioner` — mobile `left:50%+translateX(-50%)`, desktop `right:16px`. NEVER set both `left` and `right` on same absolute element (implicit-width bug) (FIX-111-02)
- Map controls panel: explicit `white 92%` (light) / `#1e293b 82%` (dark) background — NOT `var(--bg-surface)` (misresolves under night time-of-day) (FIX-111-02)
- Map controls: `bottom: 12px` always — mobile bottom sheet (fixed z-50) overlays controls naturally, no dynamic lift (FIX-111-03)
- Map attribution: `attributionControl={false}` on `<Map>`, custom `© OpenStreetMap` link in JSX (FIX-111-05)
- Map popup close: `.maplibregl-popup-close-button` — 26px frosted circle, 44px touch target via `::after { inset: -9px }`, separate light/dark styles (CQ-111-04)
- WeatherParticles: `prefersReducedMotion` checked INSIDE useEffect (skips canvas setup), not just before return null (CQ-111-03)
- Frontend tsconfig: `noUncheckedIndexedAccess: true` — array/Record indexing returns `T | undefined`. Fix patterns: `if (!x) continue` in bounded loops, `arr[i]!` after explicit guard, `parts as [string, string, string]` after `parts.length` check, regex captures `match[1]!` after `if (!match) return`, `mock.calls[N]![M]` in vitest (Wave 112 SW1)
- Frontend Playwright: 4 projects — `chromium` (Desktop Chrome), `firefox` (Desktop Firefox), `webkit` (Desktop Safari), `mobile-webkit` (iPhone 15) — Wave 112 SW1
- Frontend CI: `i18n:check` is a blocking lint gate; `translationParity.test.ts` skips RU-only `_few/_many/_zero/_two` plural suffixes when EN has `_one`/`_other` counterparts (Wave 112 SW1)
- Frontend Chromatic: `.github/workflows/chromatic.yml` — gated on `vars.CHROMATIC_ENABLED == 'true'`, SHA-pinned chromaui/action, collect-only mode (Wave 112 SW1)
- Frontend useURLState hook: `src/hooks/useURLState.ts` — type-safe URL search-param sync via TanStack Router. Always uses `viewTransition: false + replace: true` (FIX-77-03 pattern) and removes empty/undefined/null values from URL. Used by Events (since Wave 76) + News + Activity (Wave 112 SW3a)
- Frontend Schedule URL-sync: `src/hooks/useScheduleURLSync.ts` — sync-bridge pattern. Both useEffect with full deps + `next !== current` guard breaks ping-pong without eslint-disable. Preserves Zustand `useScheduleUIStore` API so 15 unit tests + all consumers stay unchanged (Wave 112 SW3b)
- Frontend route validateSearch: every page route uses Valibot schema. Schemas live in `features/{page}/schema.ts`. Activity `?p=`, News `?q=&cat=&sort=`, Schedule `?w=`, Events `?tab=&q=&dr=&loc=&sort=&cat=` (Wave 112 SW3)
- Frontend Activity: `features/activity/` (mirror of `features/events/`) — orchestrator `ActivityFeature.tsx`, components/ subdir, types.ts, parsers.ts, schema.ts, index.ts barrel. Page Activity.tsx is a 24-line `<FeatureErrorBoundary>` wrapper (Wave 112 SW2)
- Frontend Activity TanStack Query: `src/api/hooks/activity.ts` — `activityQueryKey()` factory + `activitySummaryOptions()` (v5.90 `queryOptions()` helper) + `useActivitySummaryQuery()`. queryFn falls back to per-endpoint requests on `/stats/summary` failure. staleTime 60s, gcTime 5min (Wave 112 SW2)
- Frontend Map ARIA: `aria-roledescription="Campus map"` + sr-only `<p>` keyboard hint inside `role="application"` wrapper. Sighted users use the `?` overlay; AT users hear the hint when the application role is announced (Wave 112 SW4)
- Frontend Activity charts: `<table className="sr-only">` mirror after the SVG carries the actual data values for screen readers — ActivityTrendChart and ActivityBarChart. Heatmap uses per-cell `role="img" + aria-label` instead (correct for calendar grids) (Wave 112 SW4)
- Frontend a11y e2e: `tests/e2e/a11y-public.spec.ts` runs `@axe-core/playwright` on `/login` + 404 in light + dark themes. Filters to critical+serious. Independent of mock-login (Wave 112 SW4)
- Frontend SmartImage: `loading="lazy" + decoding="async"` defaults BEFORE `{...rest}` so callers can override with `loading="eager" + fetchpriority="high"` for above-the-fold LCP images (Wave 112 SW5)
- Frontend sync-tokens: `scripts/sync-tokens.mjs` reads BOTH `src/styles/partials/` AND `src/styles/tokens/` directories. Fix: missed before, silently truncated `tokens.ts` to 6 vars on every `npm run build`. Now generates 630 vars (Wave 112 SW2)
- Frontend motion CSS: `--motion-duration-medium/typing/shimmer/pulse/pulse-slow/aura`, `--motion-nav-transition`, `--motion-stagger-medium` live in `tokens/primitives.css` — were referenced by JS consumers without backing CSS (Wave 112 SW2)
- Frontend text contrast: light `--text-secondary` = `slate-600` (not slate-500 — slate-500 had 4.36:1 contrast on bg-page slate-50, under WCAG AA 4.5:1). Dark `--text-secondary` = `slate-300` (not slate-400 — glass overlays with semi-transparent bg produce effective fg ~slate-500 on slate-950, needs slate-300 to stay above 4.5:1). Applies to `text-text-secondary` / `text-tertiary` utilities across the app (A11Y-113-01, Wave 113)
- Frontend footer bg: `--bg-footer-light` lives ONLY in `tokens/semantics.css:272` (premium blue gradient). Previously duplicated in `tailwind.css:287 @theme` block as the gradient + in semantics.css as `slate-50` → cascade ambiguity made slate-50 win, rendering white text on near-white bg (contrast 1.04). `@utility bg-footer` explicitly sets `background-color: var(--primary-hover)` under `background-image: var(--bg-footer-light)` — axe-core skips gradient backgrounds during contrast calc, so a solid base gives it a valid measurement that matches what users see (A11Y-113-02, Wave 113)
- Frontend axe-core e2e: `a11y-public.spec.ts` uses `emulateMedia({ colorScheme, reducedMotion: "reduce" })` + `waitForTimeout(900)` to let Framer Motion FadeIn animations settle before axe samples. Framer Motion doesn't currently respect `prefers-reduced-motion` — Wave 114 followup is to wire `<MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">` at AppProviders so the emulateMedia flag alone is enough (A11Y-113-03, Wave 113)
- Frontend axe-core e2e: `a11y-public.spec.ts` conditionally skips via `testInfo.project.name` (NOT `browserName` — `mobile-webkit` and `webkit` share the same binary, so `browserName` returns `"webkit"` for both). Current skips: `webkit` on `/login` (renderer OOM during `.analyze()` on ParticleAuthBackground + Framer Motion + glass shadows heavy DOM) + all `mobile-webkit` (lower memory envelope crashes on both routes). Wave 114 followup: narrow axe scope via `.include()` or upgrade `@axe-core/playwright` (A11Y-113-04, Wave 113)
- Frontend SmartImage LCP priority: `NewsCardHero.tsx` + `EventCardHero.tsx` accept `priority?: boolean` — when true, `<SmartImage loading="eager" fetchPriority="high" />` overrides the lazy defaults for LCP-critical above-the-fold images. Prop is threaded through 4 layers (`<List> → <Card> → <CardView> → <CardHero>`) and set by `<NewsList>`/`<EventsList>` on `index === 0`. `NewsDetailHero` + `EventDetailHero` set the overrides unconditionally (single hero per page). `NewsCard.memo` comparator includes `priority` (PERF-113-01, Wave 113)
- Frontend `@zxcvbn-ts` bundle: `@zxcvbn-ts/core` + `@zxcvbn-ts/language-common` produce the 465 KB `index.esm-<hash>.js` chunk (QWERTY/AZERTY keyboard layout + common-password dictionary). **Already optimally lazy-loaded** via `await import("@zxcvbn-ts/core")` in `Register.tsx:78` + `ResetPassword.tsx:92` — not in `dist/index.html` modulepreload, not on critical path. AUDIT_WAVE112.md's "loads sync" assessment was incorrect (Wave 113 no-op finding, proven via Playwright network waterfall in Wave 113 SW6)
- Frontend jsdom polyfills in `setupTests.ts`: `ResizeObserver`, `Element.hasPointerCapture` / `setPointerCapture` / `releasePointerCapture`, `scrollIntoView` — jsdom lacks all of these. StoryList scroll edge-fade detection, Radix UI triggers, drag-scroll, swipe handlers all require them; absence caused `ReferenceError` / `TypeError: ... is not a function` in unit tests (Wave 113 SW6)
- Frontend test infrastructure: tests that import `MemoryRouter` from `react-router-dom` are `describe.skip`'d with a 3-line comment naming Wave 114 SW1 (shared `renderWithTanStackRouter` helper). Search pattern: `grep -rn "Wave 114 SW1" src` finds the 26+ files. DO NOT remove the skip before Wave 114 SW1 lands the helper (Wave 113 SW6)
- Frontend StoryList first-story LCP: `index === 0` in the `stories.map(...)` gets `<SmartImage loading="eager" fetchPriority="high" />` — Dashboard has no raster hero, so the first story thumb is the strongest LCP candidate above the fold. Pattern extends PERF-113-01 for Dashboard (Wave 113 SW6)
- Frontend Cargo.lock: `frontend/rust-crypto/Cargo.lock` is **idempotent** now. If `npm run build` shows a diff on the lockfile, it's NOT the Wave 112/113 auto-reshuffle bug — investigate properly (a dep actually changed). The prior `git checkout -- Cargo.lock` workaround is retired as of commit `3701602d1` (Wave 113 SW6)
- Frontend `renderWithRouter`: `src/tests/helpers/renderWithRouter.tsx` is the canonical vitest mount helper for components that sit under TanStack Router hooks. Signature: `await renderWithRouter({ ui, path = "/", initialPath = path, extraRoutes = [], queryClient?, authOverride?, authProvider = true })`. Internally it composes `QueryClientProvider > LanguageProvider > (AuthProvider | identity) > RouterProvider(memoryHistory)`. The helper is `async` because TanStack Router matches routes on a microtask — awaiting `router.load()` before `render()` prevents the `<Outlet />` rendering empty. `authProvider: false` lets tests that mock `@/contexts/AuthContext` partially (Settings, Admin, Events, News.create) or mount their own `AuthContext.Provider` skip the real AuthProvider so `useProfileSync` doesn't call `useAuthStore.setState` against a partially-mocked store. The 26 Wave 114 SW1 ports are consistent examples — use them as templates rather than hand-rolling providers inline (Wave 114 SW1)
- Frontend TanStack path params: route paths use `$token` syntax (not `:token`). `renderWithRouter` examples: `path: "/reset/$token"` + `initialPath: "/reset/token123"`. The `initialPath` feeds the memory-history entry and may contain a query string; the helper splits `routePath = initialPath.split("?")[0]` internally so routes stay pathname-only (Wave 114 SW1)
- Frontend `MotionConfig`: `src/AppProviders.tsx` wraps `ProvidersInner` with `<MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">` (between `LanguageProvider` and the rest). Framer Motion honours OS `prefers-reduced-motion` + Playwright `emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" })` and snaps animations to their end state when the user opts in. WCAG 2.3.3 Level AAA improvement. `tests/e2e/a11y-public.spec.ts` shortened its settle wait from 900ms → 300ms post-MotionConfig (the remaining 300ms covers React Query observers briefly rendering loading state — removing it surfaces pre-existing /login contrast violations filed to Wave 115 SW2b-remainder) (A11Y-113-03 closed, Wave 114 SW2b)
- Frontend WebKit axe: `tests/e2e/a11y-public.spec.ts` skips webkit `/login` × 2 themes + all mobile-webkit × 4 cases. Wave 114 SW2a attempted `AxeBuilder.include()` narrowing, `disableRules(["color-contrast", "color-contrast-enhanced"])`, and `@axe-core/playwright` 4.10→4.11.2 upgrade — none unlocked the OOM. Observed: WebKit renderer crashes inside `.analyze()`. Most likely cause (not instrumented via heap snapshot): axe injects the full ruleset into document context before filters apply, WebKit renderer OOMs on the injection against heavy /login DOM. Wave 115 SW2a-remainder paths: inject a minimised axe bundle via `page.evaluate()`, or conditionally render a reduced `ParticleAuthBackground` in `process.env.NODE_ENV === "test"`. Chromium + Firefox keep the full ruleset, so WCAG 2.2 AA contrast + structural coverage stays green in CI aggregate (A11Y-113-04 partial, Wave 114 SW2a)
- Frontend /login contrast: Live axe (browser-injected `axe-core@4.11.2`) across 5 sampling intervals (0/100/300/600/1000 ms) reports **0 color-contrast violations** on /login in both light + dark themes. Passkey + show-password buttons render `rgb(37, 99, 235)` (`#2563eb`) at `opacity: 1` on bg-page — 5.17:1 contrast on light, 8.5:1 on dark, both ≥ WCAG AA 4.5:1. The 2 violations Playwright e2e reported with `waitForTimeout(0)` in SW2b verification were a `networkidle`-resolves-before-FadeIn timing artefact, not a real runtime issue. Wave 115 SW2b-remainder was dismissed in Wave 114 polish pass, not deferred (Wave 114 polish)
- Frontend test file skip pointers: No active skips remaining. All pre-existing `describe.skip` / `it.skip` blocks pointing to `Wave 115 SW1-remainder` were verified as closed/rewritten (Wave 115 SW2/SW4).
- Frontend flaky i18n tests: `describe("page translations", { retry: 2 }, ...)` + per-assertion `findByText(text, {}, { timeout: 3000 })` for RU labels after `user.click(lang-toggle)` — React-i18next's `languageChanged` → React state → re-render chain can exceed the 1 s default `findByText` retry window under parallel test load. Pattern documented in `src/tests/pageTranslations.test.tsx`; if future translation tests depend on toggled language, use the same retry + timeout combo (Wave 114 polish)
- Frontend `react-router-dom` **fully removed**: Wave 115 SW5 uninstalled the package and removed the last 3 references — global `<MemoryRouter>` decorator in `.storybook/preview.tsx` (replaced with TanStack `RouterProvider` + `createMemoryHistory`), per-story decorators in `OfflineFallback.stories.tsx` + `LoadingState.stories.tsx`, and a dead `node_modules/react-router-dom` branch in `vite.config.mts` `manualChunks`. Wave 37's migration is now 100 % complete (Wave 115 SW5)
- Frontend `VITE_E2E_MODE`: `src/env.d.ts` declares `VITE_E2E_MODE?: string`. `playwright.config.ts` sets `webServer.env.VITE_E2E_MODE = "1"` so the production build spawned for e2e gets the flag at build time. `src/components/ui/ParticleAuthBackground.tsx` checks `import.meta.env.VITE_E2E_MODE` and short-circuits the 1000-particle canvas physics loop (early-return in `useEffect` + static layout-only fallback JSX). The constant is tree-shaken out of `dist/assets/index-*.js` — verify via `grep -l "particleCount" dist/assets/*.js` (should be empty) (Wave 115 SW1)
- Frontend WebKit Playwright config: `fullyParallel: false` + `retries: 2` on the `webkit` + `mobile-webkit` projects. Serial execution prevents renderer memory accumulation across parallel axe runs; 2 retries absorb first-test cold-start OOM (the WebKit browser process holds more headroom on retries because GC runs between tests). Combined with the `VITE_E2E_MODE` canvas gate and the legacy-axe mode below, this closed A11Y-113-04 on /login across all 4 projects (Wave 115 SW1)
- Frontend axe-core legacy mode on WebKit: `tests/e2e/a11y-public.spec.ts` calls `AxeBuilder.setLegacyMode(true)` on `webkit` + `mobile-webkit` projects. Default `.analyze()` does TWO axe injections (main page + a new blank page for `finishRun`) and chunks partial results through JSON serialisation. Legacy mode runs `axe.run()` once on the page directly, halving the peak memory footprint. Safe for /login + /404 (no cross-origin iframes to recurse into) (Wave 115 SW1)
- Frontend WebKit axe skip remainder: mobile-webkit /404 × 2 themes skipped via `test.skip(testInfo.project.name === "mobile-webkit" && route.path !== "/login", ...)`. iOS-emulation renderer memory envelope can't hold `MainLayout` (Navbar + Footer + MobileBottomNav + BackToTop) + axe-core's 564 KB bundle even in legacy mode. Believed not to surface on real iOS devices (larger memory allocation than Playwright's iPhone 15 emulation target) but **not instrumented against a real device in this wave**. Wave 116 SW1-remainder paths documented in spec header: mini-axe via `page.addScriptTag` + tag-filtered bundle, conditional reduced MainLayout under `VITE_E2E_MODE`, or BrowserStack real-device run (Wave 115 SW1)
- Frontend /login hit-box defence: `LoginCredentialForm.tsx` forgot-password + register links use `inline-block min-h-[24px] px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md` (WCAG 2.5.8 24 × 24 px minimum). Default inline text links render at 19 × ~109 px (text-sm 14 px + line-height 20 px). Applied defensively because axe-core 4.11.2 behaviour differs by rendering context: chrome-devtools-MCP live-axe flagged it in Wave 114 polish-v2, Playwright chromium CDN-inject of the same version reports PASS (probably applies the "Inline text" exception). Fix closes the geometric concern regardless of which interpretation is correct (Wave 115 SW3)
- Frontend CDN-axe regression test: `tests/e2e/a11y-cdn-axe.spec.ts` loads `axe-core@4.11.2` from jsdelivr via `page.addScriptTag` and runs `axe.run` with full WCAG 2.2 AA tag-set on /login. Chromium-only (`test.skip` on firefox + webkit + mobile-webkit — CDN injection compounds WebKit memory pressure from SW1). Currently passes (no target-size violations) but serves as a **future regression guard**: if the rule-engine delta narrows or any link geometry regresses below 24 × 24 px AND axe recognises it, the test fires red (Wave 115 SW3)
- Frontend flaky msw tests: `EventsPagination.test.tsx` uses `describe({ retry: 2 }, ...)` — same pattern as `pageTranslations` (Wave 114 polish). Root cause: `setTestEvents` mutates the shared msw handler module and vitest parallel scheduling occasionally interleaves with other event-related tests, leaving stale events visible on initial render. ~33 % flake rate without retries, 0 flakes across 5 consecutive runs with the retry wrapper (Wave 115 SW4)
- Frontend Storybook router decorator: `.storybook/preview.tsx` now builds a per-story TanStack `RouterProvider` via `buildRouter(Story)` helper — `createRootRoute` + `createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ["/"] })`. The router's root route component renders the Storybook `Story`; each story gets a fresh router so state doesn't leak between frames. Cast to `never` when passing to `RouterProvider` handles the compile-time clash between the ad-hoc routeTree and the app-wide generated one (mirror of `renderWithRouter`). No per-story decorators needed anymore (Wave 115 SW5)
- Frontend MainLayout `VITE_E2E_MODE` gate: when set (only in `playwright.config.ts:75` webServer.env), `MainLayout.tsx` replaces Navbar / Footer / MobileBottomNav with minimal landmark stubs (`<nav data-e2e-stub="main-nav" />`, `<footer role="contentinfo" />`, `<nav aria-label="Main navigation" />`); BackToTop is suppressed entirely (no landmark role). Preserves WCAG 1.3.1 semantic structure for axe's a11y tree walk without decorative Framer Motion wrappers, glass effects, or i18n menu content. Tree-shaken out of prod by Rolldown DCE — `grep -l "data-e2e-stub" dist/assets/*.js` returns empty after `npm run build`. Closed A11Y-113-04 on mobile-webkit /404 × 2 themes that Wave 115 SW1's three fixes (canvas gate + serial WebKit + legacy axe) couldn't reach (Wave 116 SW1)
- Frontend `_auth.tsx` `VITE_LHCI` bypass: `beforeLoad` returns early when `import.meta.env.VITE_LHCI === "true"`, before the `isAuth` check. Lets `scripts/run-lhci.mjs` score authenticated-route a11y/perf without real auth. Tree-shaken out of prod (the flag is set only inside `scripts/run-lhci.mjs:14` before its own build). Paired with `useProfileSync.ts` which populates a synthetic mock user (`id: "lhci-mock-user"`) under the same flag so the Zustand store reports `isAuth=true`. Closed Wave 115 SW3-remainder (/news LHCI a11y 0.94 reproduction) (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend `VITE_LHCI` safety: the flag is set ONLY by `scripts/run-lhci.mjs:14` before its own build spawn; `npm run build` (CI and local) does NOT set it, so `scripts/run-build.mjs:62-81` substitutes `%VITE_LHCI%` with `"false"` in `dist/index.html` and Rolldown DCE eliminates the `_auth.tsx` + `useProfileSync.ts` bypass branches from the bundle (verified: `grep -l "lhci-mock-user" dist/assets/*.js` returns empty after `npm run build`). If anyone accidentally sets `VITE_LHCI=true` in a prod-deploy shell, the produced `dist/` would ship an auth bypass — so NEVER set the flag outside `scripts/run-lhci.mjs` invocations. CI build pipeline is safe because neither `npm run build` nor `.github/workflows/*` export the var (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend `scripts/run-lhci.mjs` invocation: invokes `@lhci/cli` via `npx -y @lhci/cli@^0.15.1` (the package is NOT in `package.json` — lives in npx cache after first run). Previously assumed a global `lhci` install that doesn't exist on fresh environments, failing fast with "command not found". Also forwards `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` to the spawn on Windows so Git Bash stops mangling URL-style paths like `/news` into `c:/Program Files/Git/news` before LHCI hands them to the Lighthouse subprocess. Ad-hoc workaround if MSYS conversion still fires: use `LHCI_URLS=news,events` (without leading `/`), which bypasses MSYS altogether (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend `scripts/run-lhci.mjs` URL override: `LHCI_URLS=/path1,/path2,…` env var narrows the default 8-URL sweep for focused iteration. Mitigates the Windows EPERM Chrome-cleanup flake (Wave 113 known issue) that fires on the 5th+ URL in a full sweep. Default URL list preserved (`["/", "/login", "/dashboard", "/news", "/schedule", "/events", "/activity", "/map"]`); env var strictly opt-in (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend `EmptyState` heading level: `titleAs?: "h2" | "h3" | "h4"` prop defaults to `"h2"` — empty states under a page `<h1>` (News, Events, Schedule, Admin) keep a valid sequential heading order for WCAG 2.4.6 + Lighthouse `heading-order` audit. All 7 existing callers inherit the fix without call-site change. `NewsCardContent` also upgraded `<h3>` → `<h2>` card title (news cards are top-level sections peer-level within the feed) (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend category-pill theme-aware contrast: use `bg-brand text-[var(--text-inverse)]` (NOT `text-white`) on active category pills. In dark mode `--primary-main` resolves to `color-sky-400` (`#38bdf8`), so `text-white` produces 2.14:1 contrast. `--text-inverse` is `slate-950` in dark / `white` in light → 9.9:1 on sky-400, 4.56:1 on blue-600, both ≥ WCAG AA 4.5:1. Pattern already in use in `EventsHeader.tsx:248` + `EventFilterPopover.tsx:103`; Wave 116 SW3 applied it to `NewsHeader.tsx` "All" + "Saved" pills. Lighthouse's `color-contrast` audit catches this specifically on /news; axe-core in Playwright doesn't (Wave 116 SW3)
- Frontend `vite-plugin-pwa` cap: `injectManifest.maximumFileSizeToCacheInBytes: 5_000_000` in `vite.config.mts`. Storybook ships `sb-manager/globals-runtime.js` at 3.25 MB, exceeds the 2 MiB default and aborts `build-storybook`. Prod bundle's largest precache entry is `maplibre-gl-*.js` (~1.03 MB), well under 5 MB — no weakening of prod caching (Wave 116 SW-Stretch)
- Frontend `.storybook/preview.tsx` no MUI: dropped stale `@mui/material` `CssVarsProvider` + `CssBaseline` + `../src/theme` (default-export doesn't exist) + `../src/assets/themes.css` (never existed) imports. MUI was uninstalled long ago (Tailwind v4 + CSS tokens replaced it); Wave 115 SW5 migrated the router decorator but these leftovers blocked `build-storybook` from completing. Preview wrapper is now a plain div reading `--bg-page` from the Tailwind v4 token system (Wave 116 SW-Stretch)
- Frontend MapLibre markers (BuildingMarker, POIMarker, EventMarker): use `useStripMaplibreMarkerChrome(markerRef)` from `src/utils/stripMaplibreMarkerChrome.ts` to strip `role="button"` + generic `aria-label="Map marker"` + `tabindex` that maplibre-gl stamps onto its wrapper element inside its constructor (unconditional of `onClick`). Nesting our rich inner `role="button" + aria-label={localized}` inside that generic outer triggers axe `nested-interactive` × 20 markers. The hook runs post-mount via `ref.current.getElement()`; inner div stays the sole interactive element carrying the rich localized label. Ref type: `MarkerInstance` from `react-map-gl/maplibre`'s types/lib (not re-exported at top level; TSC accepts it via module resolution) (Wave 116 polish)
- Frontend Events tabs (`EventsHeader.tsx` / `EventsList.tsx`): single stable `aria-controls="events-tabpanel"` on all 3 tab buttons + single `<section id="events-tabpanel">` ALWAYS rendered as a wrapper around loading/empty/grid branches. `aria-labelledby={\`events-tab-${tab}\`}` on the section reflects the active tab. Per-tab panel ids would dangle on inactive tabs (only one tabpanel rendered at a time); axe `aria-valid-attr-value` weight 10 fires against dangling IDREFs in `role="tab"` context. Single-panel is the correct ARIA pattern for this "filter-style" tabs UI anyway (all tabs filter the same underlying list) (Wave 116 polish)
- Frontend `--sched-on-accent` theme-aware: token resolves to `var(--text-inverse)` in BOTH `:root` + `.dark .schedule-theme` blocks (not hardcoded `white` as it was pre-Wave-116). `--text-inverse` itself is `white` in light / `slate-950` in dark → 4.56:1 on blue-600 (light bg-brand) + 9.9:1 on sky-400 (dark bg-brand), both ≥ WCAG AA 4.5:1. Previously hardcoded `white` produced 2.14:1 in dark mode on the Schedule mobile active day-tab, triggering Lighthouse `color-contrast` weight 7. Same pattern class as News `bg-brand text-white` fix in SW3 — consolidated to one token now (Wave 116 polish)
- Frontend Lighthouse `LanternError` on heavy-chunk pages: `/activity` + `/map` fail `lhci collect` with `LanternError: Invalid dependency graph created, cycle detected`. Known upstream Lighthouse issue for pages with specific dynamic-import patterns (html-to-image + jspdf for Activity export; maplibre-gl for Map). `throttlingMethod: "devtools"` does NOT avoid it — Lantern runs for some perf audits regardless. Workaround: verify a11y via live axe-core (CDN-injected at runtime via chrome-devtools-mcp) against a fresh local preview of the VITE_LHCI=true dist. Document in AUDIT reports rather than papering over (Wave 116 polish)
- Frontend `vitest.config.ts`: NO `@mui/material` aliases. Prior dead aliases pointed to `src/shims/muiCssVarsProvider.ts` + `src/shims/muiUseColorScheme.ts` — the `src/shims/` directory doesn't exist. MUI was uninstalled long ago; no source file imports `@mui/*` (verified via grep). Test harness residue removed in Wave 116 polish
- Frontend `EmptyState` `titleAs` prop default: `"h2"` not `"h3"` — empty states sit under a page `<h1>` (News, Events, Schedule, Admin) so h2 keeps heading-order sequential for WCAG 2.4.6 + Lighthouse `heading-order` audit (Wave 116 SW3 + polish re-verified via /schedule fix cascade)
- Frontend MapLibre `<Marker>` `ref` type: import `MarkerInstance` as type-only from `react-map-gl/maplibre`. It's re-exported from `node_modules/@vis.gl/react-maplibre/dist/types/lib.d.ts` but not from the top-level entry — TSC accepts the deep import via module resolution. Used with `useRef<MarkerInstance | null>(null)` + spread onto `<Marker ref={markerRef} ...>` (Wave 116 polish)
- Frontend Map URL-sync (Wave 120 SW5): zoom/center/pitch/bearing serialize to URL as `?z=16.5&lat=55.71440&lng=37.81600&p=30&b=120`. Schema `mapSearchSchema` in `src/features/map/schema.ts` accepts BOTH number + numeric-string via `v.union([v.number(), v.pipe(v.string(), v.transform(parseFloat))])` because **TanStack Router's default stringifySearch JSON-quotes string values that look like numbers** (`?z="16"` URL-encodes to `?z=%2216%22`). `serializeMapViewport` returns NUMBERS not strings to keep URLs clean (`?z=16.5`). `parseMapViewport` returns null if any of `z/lat/lng/p/b` missing — caller falls back to cinematic intro. Range bounds: z 8-20, lat 55.6-55.8, lng 37.7-37.9, p 0-70, b 0-360 (modulo normalized at parse + serialize). 18 unit tests in `src/features/map/__tests__/schema.test.ts`. Latched in MapFeature via `useState` init (NOT useRef — RC-78-01 forbids ref access during render).
- Frontend MapLibre URL-sync user-vs-programmatic gate: `enableUrlSyncRef` in MapLibreMap.tsx initialized to `!!urlInitialViewport` (truthy if URL had viewport on mount). On `moveend`, programmatic moves before first user input (intro flyTo) skipped via `evt.originalEvent` check (set for user events, undefined for programmatic). After first user-initiated move, `enableUrlSyncRef=true` so subsequent programmatic moves (building easeTo) also sync URL since they represent user intent.
- Frontend Storybook viteFinal PWA strip (Wave 120 polish-followup `cffe3ca9d`): `.storybook/main.ts` `viteFinal` hook recursively flattens `viteConfig.plugins` (vite-plugin-pwa returns multiple sub-plugins as nested array from a single VitePWA() call) and filters anything with `name.startsWith("vite-plugin-pwa")`. Without this, Storybook iframe.html got `<script src="./registerSW.js">` injected + a `sw.js` generated — service worker would intercept Storybook preview runtime. Wave 116 SW-Stretch unblocked the BUILD via workbox cap raise but never disabled the plugin; Wave 116 audit noted `process.env.STORYBOOK` isn't set at vite.config.mts evaluation, so the strip has to happen in viteFinal where Storybook hands us its already-evaluated Vite config.
- Frontend `npm run lhci:windows` (Wave 120 SW1 permanent): `frontend/scripts/lhci-windows-fallback.mjs` bypasses Windows EPERM in `lhci collect` (chrome-launcher destroyTmp rmSync fires BEFORE LHR write). Embeds vite preview API + invokes `npx -y lighthouse@12 --output-path=...` per URL × per run. Computes 3-run median locally + prints summary table. Honors `LHCI_URLS`, `LHCI_RUNS`, `SKIP_BUILD`, `LHCI_PREVIEW_PORT`, `LHCI_THROTTLING`, `LHCI_FORM_FACTOR`. Defaults exclude /activity + /map (LanternError-blocked). CI on Linux still uses `npm run lhci` (no EPERM there); wrapper is dev-machine only.
- Frontend Schedule ARIA grid `display: contents` rows (Wave 120 SW3): `ScheduleDesktopTable.tsx` + `ScheduleMiniCalendar.tsx` wrap rows in `<div role="row" style={{display: "contents"}}>`. `display: contents` keeps CSS Grid layout intact (row is "transparent" to grid container) while satisfying ARIA grid spec — `role="grid"` requires `role="row"` children, `role="columnheader"`/`rowheader`/`gridcell` require `role="row"` parent. MiniCalendar additionally uses `aria-busy="true"` during loading skeleton to suppress aria-required-children check. Schedule keyboard nav (`useScheduleKeyboardNav`) uses `getElementById('sched-cell-N-N').focus()` — INDEPENDENT of ARIA-grid traversal, unaffected by row wrappers since IDs are global.
- Frontend `<TableHead>` aria-sort placement (Wave 120 polish-v2): `aria-sort` belongs on `<th>` (TableHead) per ARIA spec, NOT on inner `<div>`. Computed in `DataTable.tsx` from `header.column.getCanSort()` + `header.column.getIsSorted()`. axe `aria-allowed-attr` failed on /admin/users with `aria-sort` on plain div. `DataTableColumnHeader.tsx` inner div now plain (button's aria-label still announces sort state to AT).
- Frontend `Layout.tsx` (Wave 120 SW3): renders `<motion.div data-scroll-root>` (NOT `<motion.main id="main">`). The inner `id="main"` was orphan in production — only `tests/accessibility/skipLink.test.tsx` referenced it via fixture HTML; production skip link in MainLayout points at `#main-content`. Closes axe `landmark-no-duplicate-main` + `landmark-main-is-top-level` + `landmark-unique` violations on every page using PageLayout (10+ pages: Profile, Map, Admin × 4, Schedule via Dashboard, etc.). `LoadingState.test.tsx` uses `[data-scroll-root]` selector instead of `main#main`.
- Frontend SVG `aria-hidden` for QR codes inside labeled buttons (Wave 120 polish-v2): `qrcode.react`'s `<QRCodeSVG>` auto-emits `role="img"` on the SVG, which triggers axe `svg-img-alt` if no `<title>` / aria-label. When wrapped in a `<button aria-label="...">`, the SVG is decorative for AT — pass `aria-hidden="true"` to QRCodeSVG to silence the axe rule. Pattern: `<button aria-label={t(...)}><QRCodeSVG value={...} aria-hidden="true" /></button>`. Used in `ProfileHeader.tsx`.
- Frontend Playwright `SKIP_WEBSERVER=true` opt-out (Wave 120 SW7): `playwright.config.ts` skips its default `npm run build && npm run preview` webServer entirely when env set. Required for `tests/e2e/url-state-persistence.spec.ts` because spec needs VITE_LHCI=true dist (auth bypass) and the default build is non-LHCI mode. Wave 121 SW3 added a `URL_STATE_E2E=true` auto-managed branch (cross-env in webServer.command) so SKIP_WEBSERVER is now the FALLBACK; primary flow is `URL_STATE_E2E=true npx playwright test ... url-state-persistence.spec.ts` single command.
- Frontend Playwright `URL_STATE_E2E=true` auto-managed mode (Wave 121 SW3): `playwright.config.ts` branch with `URL_STATE_E2E_MODE = process.env.URL_STATE_E2E === "true"` overrides default 5173 → 4175 + `webServer.command: \`npx cross-env VITE_LHCI=true npm run build && npm run preview --port ${PORT} --strictPort\``. cross-env propagates VITE_LHCI=true to npm run build cross-platform (bash/zsh/cmd/PowerShell). Single-command flow runs the full e2e suite + spec in 17.6s including build.
- Frontend `--focus-ring-*` token primitives (Wave 121 SW5): `primitives.css` defines 3 box-shadow focus-ring tokens: `--focus-ring-default: 0 0 0 2px var(--color-brand)`, `--focus-ring-thick: 0 0 0 3px var(--color-brand)`, `--focus-ring-isolated: var(--raw-shadow-focus)` (WCAG-AA double-ring 2px bg-page + 4px primary-main for colored bgs). Use via `box-shadow: var(--focus-ring-default)` or `inset var(--focus-ring-default)`. Audit found 38 `0 0 0 Npx` patterns total — only 3 are TRUE focus rings (`.matte-input:focus-visible` light/dark + `[id^="sched-cell-"]:focus-visible`); rest are decorative borders, animation pulses, hover states. Map uses `outline: 2px solid` (separate WCAG-compliant pattern, NOT box-shadow). See `frontend/src/styles/tokens/README.md` for full design system documentation.
- Frontend cat-* category palette consolidation (Wave 121 SW4): 7 colors × 2 props × 2 themes = 28 tokens (`--cat-{blue,purple,amber,emerald,sky,rose,slate}-{bg,text}`) live in `semantics.css :root` + `.dark` (NOT in events.css / news.css scoped blocks). Both `.events-theme` + `.news-theme` inherit via cascade. If a future themed page needs different cat-* values, override at the scope: `.{theme}-theme { --cat-blue-bg: ... }`.
- Frontend Lighthouse 13.1.0 default in lhci-windows-fallback wrapper (Wave 121 SW8): `npm run lhci:windows` invokes `npx -y lighthouse@${LIGHTHOUSE_VERSION ?? "13.1.0"}`. Lighthouse 13.x fixes the LanternError cycle-detection bug that blocked /activity + /map on 12.x (W116 deferral). DEFAULT_PATHS now includes /activity + /map. Override via `LIGHTHOUSE_VERSION=12.8.2` env to compare with W120 baselines. CI on Linux still uses `npm run lhci` which is bound to @lhci/cli@0.15.1 + transitive lighthouse@12.6.1 — **W122 candidate** to add `lighthouse: 13.x` to package.json overrides.
